## Physical sanity

Per-candidate physical plausibility of the strong rows above. T0 mid = start of the BLS box + duration/2 (BJD_TDB = BTJD + 2457000). Rp = sqrt(depth) x R* x 109.08 is a LOWER BOUND (blend dilution only deepens the true transit).
